href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n</h4>\n\n🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n\n⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Foundry, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n\n- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.\n- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.\n- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.\n- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.\n- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.\n\n![Debug Contracts tab](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/assets/55535804/b237af0c-5027-4849-a5c1-2e31495cccb1)\n\n## Requirements\n\nBefore you begin, you need to install the following tools:\n\n- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)\n- Yarn ([v1](https://classic.yarnpkg.com/en/docs/install/) or [v2+](https://yarnpkg.com/getting-started/install))\n- [Git](https://git-scm.com/downloads)\n\n## Quickstart\n\nTo get started with Scaffold-ETH 2, follow the steps below:\n\n1. Install dependencies if it was skipped in CLI:\n\n```\ncd my-dapp-example\nyarn install\n```\n\n2. Run a local network in the first terminal:\n\n```\nyarn chain\n```\n\nThis command starts a local Ethereum network using Foundry. The network runs on your local machine and can be used for testing and development. You can customize the network configuration in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`.\n\n3. On a second terminal, deploy the test {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CRjnPtvRyJdgxPVZsxZLhn","type":"tool_result","content":"//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\n// Useful for debugging. Remove when deploying to a live network.\nimport \"forge-std/console.sol\";\n\n// Use openzeppelin to inherit battle-tested implementations (ERC20, ERC721, etc)\n// import \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\n\n/**\n * A smart contract that allows changing a state variable of the contract and tracking the changes\n * It also allows the owner to withdraw the Ether in the contract\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract YourContract {\n  // State Variables\n  address public immutable owner;\n  string public greeting = \"Building Unstoppable Apps!!!\";\n  bool public premium = false;\n  uint256 public totalCounter = 0;\n  mapping(address => uint256) public userGreetingCounter;\n\n  // Events: a way to emit log statements from smart contract that can be listened to by external parties\n  event GreetingChange(\n    address indexed greetingSetter,\n    string newGreeting,\n    bool premium,\n    uint256 value\n  );\n\n  // Constructor: Called once on contract deployment\n  // Check packages/foundry/deploy/Deploy.s.sol\n  constructor(address _owner) {\n    owner = _owner;\n  }\n\n  // Modifier: used to define a set of rules that must be met before or after a function is executed\n  // Check the withdraw() function\n  modifier isOwner() {\n    // msg.sender: predefined variable that represents address of the account that called the current function\n    require(msg.sender == owner, \"Not the Owner\");\n    _;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows anyone to change the state variable \"greeting\" of the contract and increase the counters\n   *\n   * @param _newGreeting (string memory) - new greeting to save on the contract\n   */\n  function setGreeting(string memory _newGreeting) public payable {\n    // Print data to the anvil chain console. Remove when deploying to a live network.\n\n    console.logString(\"Setting new greeting\");\n    console.logString(_newGreeting);\n\n    greeting = _newGreeting;\n    totalCounter += 1;\n    userGreetingCounter[msg.sender] += 1;\n\n    // msg.value: built-in global variable that represents the amount of ether sent with the transaction\n    if (msg.value > 0) {\n      premium = true;\n    } else {\n      premium = false;\n    }\n\n    // emit: keyword used to trigger an event\n    emit GreetingChange(msg.sender, _newGreeting, msg.value > 0, msg.value);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows the owner to withdraw all the Ether in the contract\n   * The function can only be called by the owner of the contract as defined by the isOwner modifier\n   */\n  function withdraw() public isOwner {\n    (bool success,) = owner.call{ value: address(this).balance }(\"\");\n    require(success, \"Failed to send Ether\");\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows the contract to receive ETH\n   */\n  receive() external payable { }\n}\n===DEPLOY===\n//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\nimport { DeployYourContract } from \"./DeployYourContract.s.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice Main deployment script for all contracts\n * @dev Run this when you want to deploy multiple contracts at once\n *\n * Example: yarn deploy # runs this script(without`--file` flag)\n */\ncontract DeployScript is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n  function run() external {\n    // Deploys all your contracts sequentially\n    // Add new deployments here when needed\n\n    \n    DeployYourContract deployYourContract = new DeployYourContract();\n    deployYourContract.run();\n\n\n    // Deploy another contract\n    // DeployMyContract myContract = new DeployMyContract();\n    // myContract.run();\n  }\n}===DEPLOYYOUR===\n// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\nimport \"../contracts/YourContract.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice Deploy script for YourContract contract\n * @dev Inherits ScaffoldETHDeploy which:\n *      - Includes forge-std/Script.sol for deployment\n *      - Includes ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner modifier\n *      - Provides `deployer` variable\n * Example:\n * yarn deploy --file DeployYourContract.s.sol  # local anvil chain\n * yarn deploy --file DeployYourContract.s.sol --network optimism # live network (requires keystore)\n */\ncontract DeployYourContract is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n    /**\n     * @dev Deployer setup based on `ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT` in `.env`:\n     *      - \"scaffold-eth-default\": Uses Anvil's account #9 (0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720), no password prompt\n     *      - \"scaffold-eth-custom\": requires password used while creating keystore\n     *\n     * Note: Must use ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner modifier to:\n     *      - Setup correct `deployer` account and fund it\n     *      - Export contract addresses & ABIs to `nextjs` packages\n     */\n    function run() external ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner {\n        new YourContract(deployer);\n    }\n}","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ee6c09f-05f4-414a-b582-02feea8e809f","uuid":"d6570f86-45d9-4e54-978d-42e643d7b9a2","timestamp":"2026-07-24T16:34:02.797Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\n// Useful for debugging. Remove when deploying to a live network.\nimport \"forge-std/console.sol\";\n\n// Use openzeppelin to inherit battle-tested implementations (ERC20, ERC721, etc)\n// import \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\n\n/**\n * A smart contract that allows changing a state variable of the contract and tracking the changes\n * It also allows the owner to withdraw the Ether in the contract\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract YourContract {\n  // State Variables\n  address public immutable owner;\n  string public greeting = \"Building Unstoppable Apps!!!\";\n  bool public premium = false;\n  uint256 public totalCounter = 0;\n  mapping(address => uint256) public userGreetingCounter;\n\n  // Events: a way to emit log statements from smart contract that can be listened to by external parties\n  event GreetingChange(\n    address indexed greetingSetter,\n    string newGreeting,\n    bool premium,\n    uint256 value\n  );\n\n  // Constructor: Called once on contract deployment\n  // Check packages/foundry/deploy/Deploy.s.sol\n  constructor(address _owner) {\n    owner = _owner;\n  }\n\n  // Modifier: used to define a set of rules that must be met before or after a function is executed\n  // Check the withdraw() function\n  modifier isOwner() {\n    // msg.sender: predefined variable that represents address of the account that called the current function\n    require(msg.sender == owner, \"Not the Owner\");\n    _;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows anyone to change the state variable \"greeting\" of the contract and increase the counters\n   *\n   * @param _newGreeting (string memory) - new greeting to save on the contract\n   */\n  function setGreeting(string memory _newGreeting) public payable {\n    // Print data to the anvil chain console. Remove when deploying to a live network.\n\n    console.logString(\"Setting new greeting\");\n    console.logString(_newGreeting);\n\n    greeting = _newGreeting;\n    totalCounter += 1;\n    userGreetingCounter[msg.sender] += 1;\n\n    // msg.value: built-in global variable that represents the amount of ether sent with the transaction\n    if (msg.value > 0) {\n      premium = true;\n    } else {\n      premium = false;\n    }\n\n    // emit: keyword used to trigger an event\n    emit GreetingChange(msg.sender, _newGreeting, msg.value > 0, msg.value);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows the owner to withdraw all the Ether in the contract\n   * The function can only be called by the owner of the contract as defined by the isOwner modifier\n   */\n  function withdraw() public isOwner {\n    (bool success,) = owner.call{ value: address(this).balance }(\"\");\n    require(success, \"Failed to send Ether\");\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows the contract to receive ETH\n   */\n  receive() external payable { }\n}\n===DEPLOY===\n//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\nimport { DeployYourContract } from \"./DeployYourContract.s.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice Main deployment script for all contracts\n * @dev Run this when you want to deploy multiple contracts at once\n *\n * Example: yarn deploy # runs this script(without`--file` flag)\n */\ncontract DeployScript is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n  function run() external {\n    // Deploys all your contracts sequentially\n    // Add new deployments here when needed\n\n    \n    DeployYourContract deployYourContract = new DeployYourContract();\n    deployYourContract.run();\n\n\n    // Deploy another contract\n    // DeployMyContract myContract = new DeployMyContract();\n    // myContract.run();\n  }\n}===DEPLOYYOUR===\n// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\nimport \"../contracts/YourContract.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice Deploy script for YourContract contract\n * @dev Inherits ScaffoldETHDeploy which:\n *      - Includes forge-std/Script.sol for deployment\n *      - Includes ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner modifier\n *      - Provides `deployer` variable\n * Example:\n * yarn deploy --file DeployYourContract.s.sol  # local anvil chain\n * yarn deploy --file DeployYourContract.s.sol --network optimism # live network (requires keystore)\n */\ncontract DeployYourContract is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n    /**\n     * @dev Deployer setup based on `ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT` in `.env`:\n     *      - \"scaffold-eth-default\": Uses Anvil's account #9 (0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720), no password prompt\n     *      - \"scaffold-eth-custom\": requires password used while creating keystore\n     *\n     * Note: Must use ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner modifier to:\n     *      - Setup correct `deployer` account and fund it\n     *      - Export contract addresses & ABIs to `nextjs` packages\n     */\n    function run() external ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner {\n        new YourContract(deployer);\n    }\n}","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false}}

account, uint256 amount);\n    event FailedAnvilRequest();\n\n    struct Deployment {\n        string name;\n        address addr;\n    }\n\n    string root;\n    string path;\n    Deployment[] public deployments;\n    uint256 constant ANVIL_BASE_BALANCE = 10000 ether;\n\n    /// @notice The deployer address for every run\n    address deployer;\n\n    /// @notice Use this modifier on your run() InvalidPrivateKey(\"Invalid private key\");\n        }\n        _;\n        _stopBroadcast();\n        exportDeployments();\n    }\n\n    function _startBroadcast() internal returns (address) {\n        vm.startBroadcast();\n        (, address _deployer,) = vm.readCallers();\n\n        if (block.chainid == 31337 && _deployer.balance == 0) {\n            try vm.deal(_deployer, ANVIL_BASE_BALANCE) {\n                emit AnvilSetBalance(_deployer, ANVIL_BASE_BALANCE);\n            } catch {\n                emit FailedAnvilRequest();\n            }\n        }\n        return _deployer;\n    }\n\n    function _stopBroadcast() internal {\n        vm.stopBroadcast();\n    }\n\n    function exportDeployments() internal {\n        // fetch already existing contracts\n        root = vm.projectRoot();\n        path = string.concat(root, \"/deployments/\");\n        string memory chainIdStr = vm.toString(block.chainid);\n        path = string.concat(path, string.concat(chainIdStr, \".json\"));\n\n        string memory jsonWrite;\n\n        uint256 len = deployments.length;\n\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < len; i++) {\n            vm.serializeString(jsonWrite, vm.toString(deployments[i].addr), deployments[i].name);\n        }\n\n        string memory chainName;\n\n        try vm.getChain(block.chainid) returns (Vm.Chain memory chain) {\n            chainName = chain.name;\n        } catch {\n            chainName = findChainName();\n        }\n        jsonWrite = vm.serializeString(jsonWrite, \"networkName\", chainName);\n {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015UAcERjvZEN7fn22owYyEG","type":"tool_result","content":"---\nname: openzeppelin\ndescription: \"Develop smart contracts using OpenZeppelin Contracts library. Use when the user wants to create or modify Solidity contracts that use OpenZeppelin — including token standards (ERC20, ERC721, ERC1155), access control (Ownable, AccessControl), security primitives (Pausable, ReentrancyGuard), or any OZ extension. Covers library-first integration, pattern discovery from installed source, and version-safe development.\"\n---\n\n# Develop Smart Contracts with OpenZeppelin\n\nAdapted from [OpenZeppelin's official skill](https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-skills) for Scaffold-ETH 2 Solidity projects.\n\n## Prerequisites\n\nCheck if `./packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts` exists directly in the current working directory (do not search subdirectories). If it doesn't exist, this is not a Scaffold-ETH 2 project. Follow the instructions at https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/SKILL.md to scaffold it first. If it exists, continue directly with building.\n\nDetect the Solidity framework flavor:\n- If `packages/hardhat` exists → **Hardhat flavor**\n- If `packages/foundry` exists → **Foundry flavor**\n\n## Core Principle: Prefer Library Components Over Custom Code\n\nBefore writing ANY contract logic, search the installed OpenZeppelin library for an existing component:\n\n1. **Exact match exists?** Import and use it directly — inherit, override only what's needed\n2. **Close match exists?** Import and extend — override only functions the library marks as `virtual`\n3. **No match?** Only then write custom logic. Confirm by browsing the library's directory first\n\n**Never hand-write what the library already provides:**\n- Don't write `require(totalSupply() + amount <= cap)` when `ERC20Capped` exists\n- Don't write `require(msg.sender == owner)` when `Ownable` exists\n- Don't implement token transfer hooks manually when the library's base contracts handle it\n- Don't copy library source into your contract — always import from the dependency so the project gets security updates\n\n## Pattern Discovery: Read the Installed Source\n\nAPIs, override points, and import syntax change between major versions. Don't assume patterns from memory — always verify by reading the installed source.\n\n### Step 1: Identify the installed version and browse the library\n\nFind the OpenZeppelin version and locate the installed source:\n\n- **Hardhat**: check `packages/hardhat/package.json` for `@openzeppelin/contracts` version, then browse `packages/hardhat/node_modules/@openzeppelin/contracts/`\n- **Foundry**: check `packages/foundry/lib/openzeppelin-contracts/` and the remappings in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`\n\nBrowse the library's directory structure to discover available components. Key directories inside the OZ contracts root:\n- `token/{ERC20,ERC721,ERC1155}/` — token standards and their base implementations\n- `token/{ERC20,ERC721}/extensions/` — Capped, Burnable, Pausable, Permit, Votes, Enumerable, etc.\n- `access/` — Ownable, AccessControl, AccessManager\n- `utils/` — ReentrancyGuard, Pausable, math, structs\n\n### Step 2: Read the source file for the component you need\n\nLook at:\n- **Import syntax**: how the library's own files import each other — mirror that style. OZ v5+ uses named imports:\n  ```solidity\n  // ✅ import {ERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol\";\n  // ❌ import \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol\";\n  ```\n- **`virtual` functions**: only these can be overridden. Functions that were virtual in one version may not be in another\n- **Constructor parameters**: what must be passed during deployment\n- **NatSpec comments**: `NOTE: This function is not virtual, {X} should be overridden instead` — follow these\n\n===\ndrizzle-neon\neip-5792\nerc-721\nopenzeppelin\nponder\nsiwe\nsubgraph\nx402","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ee6c09f-05f4-414a-b582-02feea8e809f","uuid":"13279552-cb1a-42d0-ba8c-72579af7dbf0","timestamp":"2026-07-24T16:34:38.717Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"---\nname: openzeppelin\ndescription: \"Develop smart contracts using OpenZeppelin Contracts library. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMCvbF3REenc59ia2EqE","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01UrY7UncfAayMddUPF6NRi9","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T163347Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol","content":"//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.20 <0.9.0;\n\nimport { IERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport { SafeERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\nimport { Ownable } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\nimport { ReentrancyGuard } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice A tip jar that accepts USDC tips and keeps an on-chain feed of them.\n * @dev The USDC token address is injected at deploy time so the same contract\n *      works against Base mainnet and a local Base fork (same token address).\n *      Tippers must `approve` this contract for `amount` before calling {tip}.\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract TipJar is Ownable, ReentrancyGuard {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    struct Tip {\n        address from;\n        uint256 amount;\n        uint256 timestamp;\n        string message;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice The USDC token this jar accepts (6 decimals on Base).\n    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n\n    /// @notice Every tip ever sent, in chronological order.\n    Tip[] public tips;\n\n    /// @notice Lifetime total of USDC tipped (in USDC's smallest unit).\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n\n    /// @notice Lifetime total tipped per address.\n    mapping(address => uint256) public tippedBy;\n\n    event NewTip(uint256 indexed id, address indexed from, uint256 amount, string message, uint256 timestamp);\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error EmptyBalance();\n\n    constructor(address _usdc, address _owner) Ownable(_owner) {\n        require(_usdc != address(0), \"USDC address required\");\n        usdc = IERC20(_usdc);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Send a USDC tip with an optional message.\n     * @dev Caller must have `approve`d this contract for at least `amount` first.\n     * @param amount USDC amount in its smallest unit (6 decimals).\n     * @param message Public message stored alongside the tip.\n     */\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external nonReentrant {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        // Pull the USDC in first, then record — measure the actual received\n        // amount so fee-on-transfer or non-standard tokens can't desync the feed.\n        uint256 balanceBefore = usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n        usdc.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n        uint256 received = usdc.balanceOf(address(this)) - balanceBefore;\n        if (received == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        uint256 id = tips.length;\n        tips.push(Tip({ from: msg.sender, amount: received, timestamp: block.timestamp, message: message }));\n        totalTipped += received;\n        tippedBy[msg.sender] += received;\n\n        emit NewTip(id, msg.sender, received, message, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Number of tips in the feed.\n    function tipCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return tips.length;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Return a slice of the tip feed, newest first.\n     * @param cursor Number of tips to skip from the newest end.\n     * @param limit Max number of tips to return.\n     */\n    function getTips(uint256 cursor, uint256 limit) external view returns (Tip[] memory page) {\n        uint256 total = tips.length;\n        if (cursor >= total) return new Tip[](0);\n\n        uint256 remaining = total - cursor;\n        uint256 size = remaining < limit ? remaining : limit;\n        page = new Tip[](size);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < size; i++) {\n            // Walk backwards from the newest, skipping `cursor` entries.\n            page[i] = tips[total - 1 - cursor - i];\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw the entire USDC balance to the owner.\n    function withdraw() external onlyOwner nonReentrant {\n        uint256 balance = usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (balance == 0) revert EmptyBalance();\n        usdc.safeTransfer(owner(), balance);\n        emit Withdrawn(owner(), balance);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":919,"cache_read_input_tokens":34125,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":919},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ee6c09f-05f4-414a-b582-02feea8e809f","uuid":"5bce7a00-d70d-4aca-a264-02441d6dfffa","timestamp":"2026-07-24T16:35:09.367Z","request_id":"req_011CdMCvZiWtPYkds2mcXPyw"}

On a local Base fork\n *      (`yarn fork base`, chain-id 31337) the fork mirrors Base state, so the\n *      same token address holds real balances — no config changes needed\n *      between local demos and a real Base deployment.\n *\n * Example:\n *   yarn deploy --file DeployTipJar.s.sol            # local anvil / Base fork\n *   yarn deploy --file DeployTipJar.s.sol --network base\n */\ncontract DeployTipJar is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n    // Circle-issued native USDC on Base mainnet.\n    address constant BASE_USDC = 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913;\n\n    function run() external ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner {\n        new TipJar(BASE_USDC, deployer);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2377,"cache_read_input_tokens":35044,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2377},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ee6c09f-05f4-414a-b582-02feea8e809f","uuid":"0f731df6-c030-4fe1-9286-00c6a9563273","timestamp":"2026-07-24T16:35:19.780Z","request_id":"req_011CdMCxPXQMBE44j5sM7JF6"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMCz378WTRh4rPW2ed69","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01DCq2iWSZDdGhrD2GMq4pTW","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T163347Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.20;\n\nimport { Test } from \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\nimport { ERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol\";\nimport { Ownable } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\nimport { TipJar } from \"../contracts/TipJar.sol\";\n\n/// @dev Minimal 6-decimal token standing in for Base USDC.\ncontract MockUSDC is ERC20 {\n    constructor() ERC20(\"USD Coin\", \"USDC\") { }\n\n    function decimals() public pure override returns (uint8) {\n        return 6;\n    }\n\n    function mint(address to, uint256 amount) external {\n        _mint(to, amount);\n    }\n}\n\ncontract TipJarTest is Test {\n    MockUSDC usdc;\n    TipJar jar;\n\n    address owner = makeAddr(\"owner\");\n    address alice = makeAddr(\"alice\");\n    address bob = makeAddr(\"bob\");\n\n    uint256 constant ONE_USDC = 1e6;\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        jar = new TipJar(address(usdc), owner);\n        usdc.mint(alice, 100 * ONE_USDC);\n        usdc.mint(bob, 100 * ONE_USDC);\n    }\n\n    function _tip(address who, uint256 amount, string memory message) internal {\n        vm.startPrank(who);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), amount);\n        jar.tip(amount, message);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_TipMovesUsdcAndRecordsFeed() public {\n        _tip(alice, 5 * ONE_USDC, \"gm\");\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(jar)), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 95 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 1);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.tippedBy(alice), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n\n        (address from, uint256 amount, uint256 timestamp, string memory message) = jar.tips(0);\n        assertEq(from, alice);\n        assertEq(amount, 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(timestamp, block.timestamp);\n        assertEq(message, \"gm\");\n    }\n\n    function test_TipEmitsEvent() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), ONE_USDC);\n        vm.expectEmit(true, true, false, true);\n        emit TipJar.NewTip(0, alice, ONE_USDC, \"hi\", block.timestamp);\n        jar.tip(ONE_USDC, \"hi\");\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_TipRevertsOnZeroAmount() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAmount.selector);\n        jar.tip(0, \"nope\");\n    }\n\n    function test_TipRevertsWithoutApproval() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        jar.tip(ONE_USDC, \"no approval\");\n    }\n\n    function test_GetTipsReturnsNewestFirst() public {\n        _tip(alice, ONE_USDC, \"first\");\n        _tip(bob, 2 * ONE_USDC, \"second\");\n        _tip(alice, 3 * ONE_USDC, \"third\");\n\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory page = jar.getTips(0, 2);\n        assertEq(page.length, 2);\n        assertEq(page[0].message, \"third\");\n        assertEq(page[1].message, \"second\");\n\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory next = jar.getTips(2, 2);\n        assertEq(next.length, 1);\n        assertEq(next[0].message, \"first\");\n\n        assertEq(jar.getTips(5, 2).length, 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_OnlyOwnerWithdraws() public {\n        _tip(alice, 10 * ONE_USDC, \"thanks\");\n\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(Ownable.OwnableUnauthorizedAccount.selector, alice));\n        jar.withdraw();\n\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        jar.withdraw();\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), 10 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(jar)), 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_WithdrawRevertsWhenEmpty() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.EmptyBalance.selector);\n        jar.withdraw();\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":882,"cache_read_input_tokens":39056,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":882},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ee6c09f-05f4-414a-b582-02feea8e809f","uuid":"59ffc52f-c3d8-4824-bd2c-36a6bfcc5166","timestamp":"2026-07-24T16:35:49.921Z","request_id":"req_011CdMCz1zAM2JoeNtWY8BT3"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMDGirzR1sCtkHS28aZ9","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01BkdrYeZ4FhNoNh117HWB73","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T163347Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/packages/nextjs/app/tipjar/_components/TipForm.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { formatUnits, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\nimport {\n  useScaffoldContract,\n  useScaffoldReadContract,\n  useScaffoldWriteContract,\n} from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst USDC_DECIMALS = 6;\n\nexport const TipForm = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress } = useAccount();\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [message, setMessage] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { data: tipJar } = useScaffoldContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeUsdc } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeTipJar, isMining } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, tipJar?.address],\n  });\n\n  // Guard against malformed input before hitting parseUnits.\n  const isValidAmount = /^\\d*\\.?\\d+$/.test(amount) && Number(amount) > 0;\n  const parsedAmount = isValidAmount ? parseUnits(amount, USDC_DECIMALS) : 0n;\n  const needsApproval = allowance !== undefined && parsedAmount > 0n && allowance < parsedAmount;\n  const hasBalance = usdcBalance === undefined || parsedAmount <= usdcBalance;\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (!tipJar?.address) return;\n    try {\n      await writeUsdc({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [tipJar.address, parsedAmount] });\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(\"Approve failed\", e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleTip = async () => {\n    if (!isValidAmount) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid USDC amount\");\n      return;\n    }\n    try {\n      await writeTipJar({ functionName: \"tip\", args: [parsedAmount, message] });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMessage(\"\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(\"Tip failed\", e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl w-full\">\n      <div className=\"card-body gap-4\">\n        <h2 className=\"card-title\">Send a tip</h2>\n\n        <label className=\"form-control w-full\">\n          <span className=\"label-text mb-1\">Amount (USDC)</span>\n          <input\n            type=\"text\"\n            inputMode=\"decimal\"\n            placeholder=\"5.00\"\n            className=\"input input-bordered w-full\"\n            value={amount}\n            onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n          />\n          {connectedAddress && usdcBalance !== undefined && (\n            <span className=\"label-text-alt mt-1 opacity-70\">\n              Balance: {formatUnits(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS)} USDC\n            </span>\n          )}\n        </label>\n\n        <label className=\"form-control w-full\">\n          <span className=\"label-text mb-1\">Message</span>\n          <input\n            type=\"text\"\n            placeholder=\"gm, thanks for the great work!\"\n            className=\"input input-bordered w-full\"\n            maxLength={280}\n            value={message}\n            onChange={e => setMessage(e.target.value)}\n          />\n        </label>\n\n        {!hasBalance && isValidAmount && (\n          <p className=\"text-error text-sm m-0\">Not enough USDC in your wallet.</p>\n        )}\n\n        {!connectedAddress ? useScaffoldContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeUsdc } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeTipJar, isMining } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, tipJar?.address],\n  });\n\n  // Guard against malformed input before hitting parseUnits.\n  const isValidAmount = /^\\d*\\.?\\d+$/.test(amount) && Number(amount) > 0;\n  const parsedAmount = isValidAmount ? parseUnits(amount, USDC_DECIMALS) : 0n;\n  const needsApproval = allowance !== undefined && parsedAmount > 0n && allowance < parsedAmount;\n  const hasBalance = usdcBalance === undefined || parsedAmount <= usdcBalance;\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (!tipJar?.address) return;\n    try {\n      await writeUsdc({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [tipJar.address, parsedAmount] });\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(\"Approve failed\", e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleTip = async () => {\n    if (!isValidAmount) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid USDC amount\");\n      return;\n    }\n    try {\n      await writeTipJar({ functionName: \"tip\", args: [parsedAmount, message] });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMessage(\"\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(\"Tip failed\", e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl w-full\">\n      <div className=\"card-body gap-4\">\n        <h2 className=\"card-title\">Send a tip</h2>\n\n        <label className=\"form-control w-full\">\n          <span className=\"label-text mb-1\">Amount (USDC)</span>\n          <input\n            type=\"text\"\n            inputMode=\"decimal\"\n            placeholder=\"5.00\"\n            className=\"input input-bordered w-full\"\n            value={amount}\n            onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n          />\n          {connectedAddress && usdcBalance !== undefined && (\n            <span className=\"label-text-alt mt-1 opacity-70\">\n              Balance: {formatUnits(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS)} USDC\n            </span>\n          )}\n        </label>\n\n        <label className=\"form-control w-full\">\n          <span {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMDgr6EuM939YFWSCuyT","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now the README with the exact local workflow:"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":242,"cache_read_input_tokens":85895,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":242,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ee6c09f-05f4-414a-b582-02feea8e809f","uuid":"3686c1b3-82f9-4925-a8c2-3a09e350b8eb","timestamp":"2026-07-24T16:44:47.409Z","request_id":"req_011CdMDgocudNETZXy9WdWMW"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMDgr6EuM939YFWSCuyT","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_017i5WMJmLarcLXwz2uW1QpU","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T163347Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/README.md","content":"# 💸 USDC Tip Jar on Base\n\nA [Scaffold-ETH 2](https://scaffoldeth.io) (Foundry flavor) dApp that accepts\n**USDC tips on Base** and shows a live, on-chain tip feed.\n\n- **Contract:** [`packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol`](packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol) — pulls USDC via `transferFrom`, records each tip (sender, amount, message, timestamp), emits `NewTip`, and lets the owner withdraw.\n- **Frontend:** [`packages/nextjs/app/tipjar`](packages/nextjs/app/tipjar) — the tip feed plus an approve → tip form.\n- **Token:** Base native USDC `0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913` (6 decimals). The address is injected at deploy time so the exact same contract works on Base mainnet and on a **local Base fork**.\n\nDeploying and shipping the static site is documented separately in [`DEPLOY.md`](DEPLOY.md).\n\n## Requirements\n\n- **Node 20 or 22 LTS** (an `.nvmrc` pins `22`; run `nvm use`). ⚠️ Do **not** use Node ≥ 23 — it ships a global `localStorage` that breaks the Next.js static export. Verified on Node 22.2.0.\n- Yarn (v3+, this repo uses `yarn@4`)\n- [Foundry](https://book.getfoundry.sh/getting-started/installation) (`anvil`, `cast`, `forge`)\n\n```bash\nnvm use          # -> Node 22\nyarn install\n```\n\n## Local development against real Base state\n\nThe team develops and demos against **real Base state** by running a local\n[Anvil fork](https://book.getfoundry.sh/anvil/) of Base. The fork mirrors\nmainnet — the real USDC contract lives at its real address with real balances —\nbut every transaction stays on your machine, so **no real money is ever at\nrisk**. Demo tips move real USDC between throwaway test identities that only\nexist on the fork.\n\nRun each step in its own terminal.\n\n### 1. Start a local Base fork\n\n```bash\nyarn fork base\n```\n\nThis runs `anvil --fork-url base --chain-id 31337`. Keeping chain-id `31337`\nmeans the frontend's default target network (`chains.foundry`) and the Anvil\ntest accounts (with 10,000 ETH each for gas) work unchanged, while contract\n_state_ is Base's. The RPC listens on `http://127.0.0.1:8545`.\n\n> `base` resolves to `https://mainnet.base.org` via `[rpc_endpoints]` in\n> `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`. Swap in your own Base RPC there for a faster,\n> rate-limit-free fork.\n\n### 2. Deploy the Tip Jar to the fork\n\n```bash\nyarn deploy\n```\n\nThis runs `script/Deploy.s.sol` (which includes `DeployTipJar.s.sol`), deploys\n`TipJar` pointed at the real Base USDC address, and regenerates\n`packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts` so the frontend picks it up.\n\n### 3. Seed test identities with USDC (fork only — not real money)\n\nThe fork has real USDC _balances_, but the Anvil test accounts start with zero\nUSDC. Mint some to them using Circle's `masterMinter` role, which you can\nimpersonate on the fork. This creates USDC that only exists on your local fork.\n\nSave this as `packages/foundry/scripts-js/seed-usdc.sh` or paste it directly —\nit mints **100 USDC to Anvil account #0 (Alice)** and **#1 (Bob)**:\n\n```bash\nRPC=http://127.0.0.1:8545\nUSDC=0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\nALICE=0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266   # anvil #0\nBOB=0x70997970C51812dc3A010C7d01b50e0d17dc79C8     # anvil #1\nALICE_PK=0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80\nBOB_PK=0x59c6995e998f97a5a0044966f0945389dc9e86dae88c7a8412f4603b6b78690d\n\n# masterMinter can authorize new minters\nMASTER=$(cast call $USDC \"masterMinter()(address)\" --rpc-url $RPC)\ncast rpc anvil_setBalance $MASTER 0xDE0B6B3A7640000 --rpc-url $RPC        # 1 ETH for gas\ncast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount $MASTER --rpc-url $RPC\ncast send $USDC \"configureMinter(address,uint256)\" $ALICE 100000000000 --from $MASTER --unlocked --rpc-url $RPC\ncast send $USDC \"configureMinter(address,uint256)\" $BOB   100000000000 --from $MASTER --unlocked --rpc-url $RPC\ncast rpc anvil_stopImpersonatingAccount $MASTER --rpc-url $RPC\n\n# Each newly-authorized minter mints 100 USDC (6 decimals) to itself\ncast send $USDC \"mint(address,uint256)\" $ALICE 100000000 --private-key $ALICE_PK --rpc-url $RPC\ncast send $USDC \"mint(address,uint256)\" $BOB   100000000 --private-key $BOB_PK   --rpc-url $RPC\n\necho \"Alice USDC:\" $(cast call $USDC \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" $ALICE --rpc-url $RPC)\necho \"Bob   USDC:\" $(cast call $USDC \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" $BOB   --rpc-url $RPC)\n```\n\n```bash\nbash packages/foundry/scripts-js/seed-usdc.sh\n```\n\n> **Why this is safe:** `anvil_impersonateAccount` and `mint` only affect the\n> local fork's state. You are not touching mainnet and cannot spend real funds.\n\n### 4. Start the frontend\n\n```bash\nyarn start\n```\n\nOpen <http://localhost:3000/tipjar>.\n\n### 5. Demo a tip between test identities\n\n1. Connect a wallet as **Alice**. The quickest path is the built-in **Burner\n   Wallet** (shown automatically on the local network) — or import Alice's\n   private key above into MetaMask and add a network for\n   `http://127.0.0.1:8545` (chain id `31337`).\n2. Enter an amount (e.g. `5`) and a message, then **Approve USDC** → **Send\n   tip**. USDC is a two-step token: the first click approves the jar to pull\n   your USDC, the second sends the tip.\n3. The tip appears in the **Tip feed** with Alice's address, amount, and\n   message; **Total tipped** and **Tips received** update.\n4. Switch to **Bob** and repeat to show tips moving between identities.\n\nThe contract owner (Anvil account #9, the deployer) can pull the collected USDC\nout with `withdraw()` — try it from the **Debug Contracts** tab.\n\n### Verify from the CLI (optional)\n\n```bash\nJAR=$(cast call <deployedContracts TipJar address> ...)   # see deployedContracts.ts\ncast call $JAR \"totalTipped()(uint256)\" --rpc-url http://127.0.0.1:8545\ncast call $JAR \"getTips(uint256,uint256)((address,uint256,uint256,string)[])\" 0 5 --rpc-url http://127.0.0.1:8545\n```\n\n## Tests\n\n```bash\nyarn foundry:test\n```\n\n`packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol` covers tipping (balance movement + feed +\nevent), zero-amount and missing-approval reverts, newest-first pagination, and\nowner-only withdraw.\n\n## Deploying to Base mainnet\n\nSame contract, real network:\n\n```bash\nyarn deploy --network base --keystore <your-keystore>\n```\n\nNo code change is needed — the USDC address is identical on the fork and on Base.\n\n---\n\nBuilt with [Scaffold-ETH 2](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io).\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":242,"cache_read_input_tokens":85895,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":242,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ee6c09f-05f4-414a-b582-02feea8e809f","uuid":"f7f38bca-0647-41e3-a2da-ed8e6224c59d","timestamp":"2026-07-24T16:45:29.359Z","request_id":"req_011CdMDgocudNETZXy9WdWMW"}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_017i5WMJmLarcLXwz2uW1QpU","type":"tool_result","content":"The file /Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T163347Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/README.md has been updated successfully. (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ee6c09f-05f4-414a-b582-02feea8e809f","uuid":"f8e49e69-8351-4f33-b32d-2662ea876580","timestamp":"2026-07-24T16:45:29.366Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"update","filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T163347Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/README.md","content":"# 💸 USDC Tip Jar on Base\n\nA [Scaffold-ETH 2](https://scaffoldeth.io) (Foundry flavor) dApp that accepts\n**USDC tips on Base** and shows a live, on-chain tip feed.\n\n- **Contract:** [`packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol`](packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol) — pulls USDC via `transferFrom`, records each tip (sender, amount, message, timestamp), emits `NewTip`, and lets the owner withdraw.\n- **Frontend:** [`packages/nextjs/app/tipjar`](packages/nextjs/app/tipjar) — the tip feed plus an approve → tip form.\n- **Token:** Base native USDC `0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913` (6 decimals). The address is injected at deploy time so the exact same contract works on Base mainnet and on a **local Base fork**.\n\nDeploying and shipping the static site is documented separately in [`DEPLOY.md`](DEPLOY.md).\n\n## Requirements\n\n- **Node 20 or 22 LTS** (an `.nvmrc` pins `22`; run `nvm use`). ⚠️ Do **not** use Node ≥ 23 — it ships a global `localStorage` that breaks the Next.js static export. Verified on Node 22.2.0.\n- Yarn (v3+, this repo uses `yarn@4`)\n- [Foundry](https://book.getfoundry.sh/getting-started/installation) (`anvil`, `cast`, `forge`)\n\n```bash\nnvm use          # -> Node 22\nyarn install\n```\n\n## Local development against real Base state\n\nThe team develops and demos against **real Base state** by running a local\n[Anvil fork](https://book.getfoundry.sh/anvil/) of Base. The fork mirrors\nmainnet — the real USDC contract lives at its real address with real balances —\nbut every transaction stays on your machine, so **no real money is ever at\nrisk**. Demo tips move real USDC between throwaway test identities that only\nexist on the fork.\n\nRun each step in its own terminal.\n\n### 1. Start a local Base fork\n\n```bash\nyarn fork base\n```\n\nThis runs `anvil --fork-url base --chain-id 31337`. Keeping chain-id `31337`\nmeans the frontend's default target network (`chains.foundry`) and the Anvil\ntest accounts (with 10,000 ETH each for gas) work unchanged, while contract\n_state_ is Base's. The RPC listens on `http://127.0.0.1:8545`.\n\n> `base` resolves to `https://mainnet.base.org` via `[rpc_endpoints]` in\n> `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`. Swap in your own Base RPC there for a faster,\n> rate-limit-free fork.\n\n### 2. Deploy the Tip Jar to the fork\n\n```bash\nyarn deploy\n```\n\nThis runs `script/Deploy.s.sol` (which includes `DeployTipJar.s.sol`), deploys\n`TipJar` pointed at the real Base USDC address, and regenerates\n`packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts` so the frontend picks it up.\n\n### 3. Seed test identities with USDC (fork only — not real money)\n\nThe fork has real USDC _balances_, but the Anvil test accounts start with zero\nUSDC. Mint some to them using Circle's `masterMinter` role, which you can\nimpersonate on the fork. This creates USDC that only exists on your local fork.\n\nSave this as `packages/foundry/scripts-js/seed-usdc.sh` or paste it directly —\nit mints **100 USDC to Anvil account #0 (Alice)** and **#1 (Bob)**:\n\n```bash\nRPC=http://127.0.0.1:8545\nUSDC=0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\nALICE=0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266   # anvil #0\nBOB=0x70997970C51812dc3A010C7d01b50e0d17dc79C8     # anvil #1\nALICE_PK=0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80\nBOB_PK=0x59c6995e998f97a5a0044966f0945389dc9e86dae88c7a8412f4603b6b78690d\n\n# masterMinter can authorize new minters\nMASTER=$(cast call $USDC \"masterMinter()(address)\" --rpc-url $RPC)\ncast rpc anvil_setBalance $MASTER 0xDE0B6B3A7640000 --rpc-url $RPC        # 1 ETH for gas\ncast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount $MASTER --rpc-url $RPC\ncast send $USDC \"configureMinter(address,uint256)\" $ALICE 100000000000 --from $MASTER --unlocked --rpc-url $RPC\ncast send $USDC \"configureMinter(address,uint256)\" $BOB   100000000000 --from $MASTER --unlocked --rpc-url $RPC\ncast rpc anvil_stopImpersonatingAccount $MASTER --rpc-url $RPC\n\n# Each newly-authorized minter mints 100 USDC (6 decimals) to itself\ncast send $USDC \"mint(address,uint256)\" $ALICE 100000000 --private-key $ALICE_PK --rpc-url $RPC\ncast send $USDC \"mint(address,uint256)\" $BOB   100000000 --private-key $BOB_PK   --rpc-url $RPC\n\necho \"Alice USDC:\" $(cast call $USDC \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" $ALICE --rpc-url $RPC)\necho \"Bob   USDC:\" $(cast call $USDC \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" $BOB   --rpc-url $RPC)\n```\n\n```bash\nbash packages/foundry/scripts-js/seed-usdc.sh\n```\n\n> **Why this is safe:** `anvil_impersonateAccount` and `mint` only affect the\n> local fork's state. You are not touching mainnet and cannot spend real funds.\n\n### 4. Start the frontend\n\n```bash\nyarn start\n```\n\nOpen <http://localhost:3000/tipjar>.\n\n### 5. Demo a tip between test identities\n\n1. Connect a wallet as **Alice**. The quickest path is the built-in **Burner\n   Wallet** (shown automatically on the local network) — or import Alice's\n   private key above into MetaMask and add a network for\n   `http://127.0.0.1:8545` (chain id `31337`).\n2. Enter an amount (e.g. `5`) and a message, then **Approve USDC** → **Send\n   tip**. USDC is a two-step token: the first click approves the jar to pull\n   your USDC, the second sends the tip.\n3. The tip appears in the **Tip feed** with Alice's address, amount, and\n   message; **Total tipped** and **Tips received** update.\n4. Switch to **Bob** and repeat to show tips moving between identities.\n\nThe contract owner (Anvil account #9, the deployer) can pull the collected USDC\nout with `withdraw()` — try it from the **Debug Contracts** tab.\n\n### Verify from the CLI (optional)\n\n```bash\nJAR=$(cast call <deployedContracts TipJar address> ...)   # see deployedContracts.ts\ncast call $JAR \"totalTipped()(uint256)\" --rpc-url http://127.0.0.1:8545\ncast call $JAR \"getTips(uint256,uint256)((address,uint256,uint256,string)[])\" 0 5 --rpc-url http://127.0.0.1:8545\n```\n\n## Tests\n\n```bash\nyarn foundry:test\n```\n\n`packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol` covers tipping (balance movement + feed +\nevent), zero-amount and missing-approval reverts, newest-first pagination, and\nowner-only withdraw.\n\n## Deploying to Base mainnet\n\nSame contract, real network:\n\n```bash\nyarn deploy --network base --keystore <your-keystore>\n```\n\nNo code change is needed — the USDC address is identical on the fork and on Base.\n\n---\n\nBuilt with [Scaffold-ETH 2](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io).\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":83,"newStart":1,"newLines":153,"lines":["-# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2","+# 💸 USDC Tip Jar on Base"," ","-<h4 align=\"center\">","-  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |","-  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>","-</h4>","+A [Scaffold-ETH 2](https://scaffoldeth.io) (Foundry flavor) dApp that accepts","+**USDC tips on Base** and shows a live, on-chain tip feed."," ","-🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n\n⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Foundry, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n\n- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.\n- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.\n- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.\n- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.\n- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.\n\n![Debug Contracts tab](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/assets/55535804/b237af0c-5027-4849-a5c1-2e31495cccb1)\n\n## Requirements\n\nBefore you begin, you need to install the following tools:\n\n- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)\n- Yarn ([v1](https://classic.yarnpkg.com/en/docs/install/) or [v2+](https://yarnpkg.com/getting-started/install))\n- [Git](https://git-scm.com/downloads)\n\n## Quickstart\n\nTo get started with Scaffold-ETH 2, follow the steps below:\n\n1. Install dependencies if it was skipped in CLI:\n\n```\ncd my-dapp-example\nyarn install\n```\n\n2. Run a local network in the first terminal:\n\n```\nyarn chain\n```\n\nThis command starts a local Ethereum network using Foundry. The network runs on your local machine and can be used for testing and development. You can customize the network configuration in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`.\n\n3. On a second terminal, deploy the test contract:\n\n```\nyarn deploy\n```\n\nThis command deploys a test smart contract to the local network. The contract is located in `packages/foundry/contracts` and can be modified to suit your needs. The `yarn deploy` command uses the deploy script located in `packages/foundry/script` to deploy the contract to the network. You can also customize the deploy script.\n\n4. On a third terminal, start your NextJS app:\n\n```\nyarn start\n```\n\nVisit your app on: `http://localhost:3000`. You can interact with your smart contract using the `Debug Contracts` page. You can tweak the app config in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.\n\nRun smart contract test with `yarn foundry:test`\n\n- Edit your smart contracts in `packages/foundry/contracts`\n- Edit your frontend homepage at `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`. For guidance on [routing](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/defining-routes) and configuring [pages/layouts](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/pages-and-layouts) checkout the Next.js documentation.\n- Edit your deployment scripts in `packages/foundry/script`\n\n\n## Documentation\n\nVisit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.\n\nTo know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).\n\n## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2\n\nWe welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!\n\nPlease see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2.","userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMDm9XLyrNNRyo7cqicb","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01GphiZMnkypKt7Yqk81eTJp","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T163347Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/packages/foundry/scripts-js/seed-usdc.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n#\n# Seed local test identities with USDC on a local Base fork.\n#\n# The fork mirrors real Base state, so the real USDC contract is present — but\n# the Anvil test accounts hold no USDC. This mints USDC to them by impersonating\n# Circle's `masterMinter` role, which is only possible on the fork. Nothing here\n# touches mainnet or real funds.\n#\n# Prereqs: a Base fork running (`yarn fork base`) on http://127.0.0.1:8545.\n# Usage:   bash packages/foundry/scripts-js/seed-usdc.sh [amount_usdc]\n#\nset -euo pipefail\n\nRPC=${RPC:-http://127.0.0.1:8545}\nUSDC=0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\nAMOUNT_USDC=${1:-100}\nAMOUNT=$((AMOUNT_USDC * 1000000)) # USDC has 6 decimals\n\n# Anvil default accounts #0 (Alice) and #1 (Bob).\nALICE=0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266\nBOB=0x70997970C51812dc3A010C7d01b50e0d17dc79C8\nALICE_PK=0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80\nBOB_PK=0x59c6995e998f97a5a0044966f0945389dc9e86dae88c7a8412f4603b6b78690d\n\nMASTER=$(cast call \"$USDC\" \"masterMinter()(address)\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\")\necho \"USDC masterMinter: $MASTER\"\n\n# Give the masterMinter gas, then impersonate it to authorize Alice & Bob as minters.\ncast rpc anvil_setBalance \"$MASTER\" 0xDE0B6B3A7640000 --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\ncast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount \"$MASTER\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\ncast send \"$USDC\" \"configureMinter(address,uint256)\" \"$ALICE\" \"$AMOUNT\" --from \"$MASTER\" --unlocked --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\ncast send \"$USDC\" \"configureMinter(address,uint256)\" \"$BOB\" \"$AMOUNT\" --from \"$MASTER\" --unlocked --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\ncast rpc anvil_stopImpersonatingAccount \"$MASTER\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\n\n# Each authorized minter mints to itself.\ncast send \"$USDC\" \"mint(address,uint256)\" \"$ALICE\" \"$AMOUNT\" --private-key \"$ALICE_PK\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\ncast send \"$USDC\" \"mint(address,uint256)\" \"$BOB\" \"$AMOUNT\" --private-key \"$BOB_PK\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\n\necho \"Alice ($ALICE) USDC: $(cast call \"$USDC\" \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" \"$ALICE\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\")\"\necho \"Bob   ($BOB) USDC: $(cast call \"$USDC\" \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" \"$BOB\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\")\"\necho \"Done. Seeded ${AMOUNT_USDC} USDC to each test identity on the fork.\"\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1107,"cache_read_input_tokens":89366,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1107,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ee6c09f-05f4-414a-b582-02feea8e809f","uuid":"2e55cbfc-a6ea-42ec-989c-342c69992cc0","timestamp":"2026-07-24T16:46:08.117Z","request_id":"req_011CdMDm8V64wUKbGNajBemC"}

This mints USDC to them by impersonating\n# Circle's `masterMinter` role, which is only possible on the fork. Nothing here\n# touches mainnet or real funds.\n#\n# Prereqs: a Base fork running (`yarn fork base`) on http://127.0.0.1:8545.\n# Usage:   bash packages/foundry/scripts-js/seed-usdc.sh [amount_usdc]\n#\nset -euo pipefail\n\nRPC=${RPC:-http://127.0.0.1:8545}\nUSDC=0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\nAMOUNT_USDC=${1:-100}\nAMOUNT=$((AMOUNT_USDC * 1000000)) # USDC has 6 decimals\n\n# Anvil default accounts #0 (Alice) and #1 (Bob).\nALICE=0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266\nBOB=0x70997970C51812dc3A010C7d01b50e0d17dc79C8\nALICE_PK=0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80\nBOB_PK=0x59c6995e998f97a5a0044966f0945389dc9e86dae88c7a8412f4603b6b78690d\n\nMASTER=$(cast call \"$USDC\" \"masterMinter()(address)\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\")\necho \"USDC masterMinter: $MASTER\"\n\n# Give the masterMinter gas, then impersonate it to authorize Alice & Bob as minters.\ncast rpc anvil_setBalance \"$MASTER\" 0xDE0B6B3A7640000 --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\ncast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount \"$MASTER\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\ncast send \"$USDC\" \"configureMinter(address,uint256)\" \"$ALICE\" \"$AMOUNT\" --from \"$MASTER\" --unlocked --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\ncast send \"$USDC\" \"configureMinter(address,uint256)\" \"$BOB\" \"$AMOUNT\" --from \"$MASTER\" --unlocked --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\ncast rpc anvil_stopImpersonatingAccount \"$MASTER\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\n\n# Each authorized minter mints to itself.\ncast send \"$USDC\" \"mint(address,uint256)\" \"$ALICE\" \"$AMOUNT\" --private-key \"$ALICE_PK\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\ncast send \"$USDC\" \"mint(address,uint256)\" \"$BOB\" \"$AMOUNT\" --private-key \"$BOB_PK\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\n\necho \"Alice ($ALICE) USDC: $(cast call \"$USDC\" \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" \"$ALICE\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\")\"\necho \"Bob   ($BOB) USDC: $(cast call \"$USDC\" \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" \"$BOB\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\")\"\necho \"Done. Seeded ${AMOUNT_USDC} USDC to each test identity on the fork.\"\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMDspeUZKoHRbVg5uW3L","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"That confirms contract names are filtered by target network — a Base-targeted build requires TipJar to be deployed on Base first (which regenerates `deployedContracts.ts`). That's the correct production ordering, and I'll make it explicit in DEPLOY.md rather than fake a mainnet deployment. The default build (fork target) is fully verified. Now let me write DEPLOY.md:"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":611,"cache_read_input_tokens":95572,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":611,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ee6c09f-05f4-414a-b582-02feea8e809f","uuid":"fdd49981-99dc-44a4-bba7-81a3aee4ca5d","timestamp":"2026-07-24T16:47:48.654Z","request_id":"req_011CdMDsn1EYGwpuyFFS2UX1"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMDspeUZKoHRbVg5uW3L","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_0174ZwRJbDtHgTBGTN1ksuc4","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T163347Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/DEPLOY.md","content":"# Deploying the Tip Jar site to IPFS\n\nThe frontend ships as a **static export** hosted on IPFS. There is no server —\n`next build` with `output: \"export\"` produces a folder of static files\n(`packages/nextjs/out/`) that any IPFS pinning service can serve.\n\nThis document lists the exact **build**, **upload**, and **post-deploy\nverification** commands. It does **not** upload anything for you.\n\n## 0. Prerequisites\n\n- **Node 20 or 22 LTS** — run `nvm use` (an `.nvmrc` pins `22`). ⚠️ Node ≥ 23\n  defines a global `localStorage` that crashes the static prerender. Verified on\n  Node 22.2.0.\n- `yarn install` has been run.\n- Foundry installed (for the contract deployment step).\n\nThe IPFS build is switched on by `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true`, which makes\n`packages/nextjs/next.config.ts` set:\n\n- `output: \"export\"` — emit a static `out/` folder\n- `trailingSlash: true` — every route becomes `route/index.html` so IPFS gateways resolve directory paths\n- `images.unoptimized: true` — no image optimization server\n\nThe target chain is switched with `NEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK` (see\n`packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`): unset → local Base fork (`chains.foundry`),\n`base` → Base mainnet (`chains.base`).\n\n---\n\n## 1. Deploy the contract to Base mainnet\n\nThe static site reads the TipJar address from\n`packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts`. That file is regenerated per\nnetwork at deploy time, so **you must deploy to Base before building the\nproduction site** — otherwise the build has no `TipJar` entry for chain `8453`.\n\n```bash\n# One-time: create/import a funded deployer keystore\nyarn account:import        # or: yarn generate\n\n# Deploy TipJar (pointed at Base USDC) to Base mainnet\nyarn deploy --network base --keystore <your-keystore>\n```\n\nThis writes the Base (`8453`) `TipJar` entry into `deployedContracts.ts`.\n\nVerify the deployment before building:\n\n```bash\nBASE_RPC=https://mainnet.base.org\nJAR=<address printed by yarn deploy>            # also in deployedContracts.ts under 8453\ncast call $JAR \"usdc()(address)\" --rpc-url $BASE_RPC\n# -> 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913  (Base USDC)\ncast call $JAR \"owner()(address)\" --rpc-url $BASE_RPC\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Build the static site\n\n```bash\nnvm use    # Node 22\n\n# Production build targeting Base mainnet, static-exported for IPFS\nNEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK=base NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true yarn next:build\n```\n\nOutput lands in `packages/nextjs/out/`. Sanity-check it locally before\nuploading:\n\n```bash\n# Every route is a directory with an index.html\nls packages/nextjs/out/tipjar/index.html packages/nextjs/out/index.html\n\n# Serve the exact static bundle and click through /tipjar\nnpx serve packages/nextjs/out\n# open http://localhost:3000/tipjar\n```\n\n> The identical export pipeline is what runs for the local fork build\n> (`NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true yarn next:build`, target unset). It has been\n> verified to emit `/tipjar` as a static page.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Upload to IPFS\n\nPick one. **Run these yourself — this repo does not upload during CI or build.**\n\n### Option A — the built-in `yarn ipfs` script (build + upload via bgipfs)\n\n```bash\n# Builds with NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true and uploads out/ to a public bgipfs gateway.\n# Add NEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK=base to point the built site at Base mainnet.\nNEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK=base yarn ipfs\n# -> prints: 🚀 Upload complete! ... https://community.bgipfs.com/ipfs/<CID>\n```\n\n### Option B — upload the already-built `out/` with the IPFS CLI (Kubo)\n\n```bash\nCID=$(ipfs add -Q -r packages/nextjs/out)\necho \"Root CID: $CID\"\n```\n\n### Option C — a pinning service (Pinata / web3.storage / Filebase)\n\nUpload the `packages/nextjs/out` folder through the service's CLI or dashboard\nand note the returned **root CID**.\n\nWhichever you use, the deliverable is the **root CID** of the `out/` directory.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Post-deploy verification\n\nReplace `<CID>` with the root CID from step 3.\n\n```bash\n# 1. The pinned bundle resolves and the tip jar route exists on a public gateway\ncurl -sfI \"https://ipfs.io/ipfs/<CID>/tipjar/index.html\" | head -1        # -> HTTP/2 200\ncurl -sfI \"https://<CID>.ipfs.dweb.link/tipjar/\"          | head -1        # -> HTTP/2 200\n\n# 2. The HTML actually rendered the app (not an error page)\ncurl -sf \"https://ipfs.io/ipfs/<CID>/tipjar/\" | grep -o \"USDC Tip Jar\"     # -> USDC Tip Jar\n\n# 3. The content is pinned somewhere (survives your local node going away)\nipfs pin ls --type=recursive | grep <CID>                                  # if self-pinning\n#    or confirm the \"pinned\" status in your pinning service dashboard\n\n# 4. Load https://ipfs.io/ipfs/<CID>/tipjar/ in a browser, connect a wallet on\n#    Base, and confirm the tip feed loads and the contract address shown matches\n#    the Base deployment from step 1.\n```\n\nOptionally set a stable name so the CID can change on each redeploy:\n\n```bash\nipfs name publish /ipfs/<CID>        # IPNS\n# or point a DNSLink TXT record: _dnslink.tips.example.com -> dnslink=/ipfs/<CID>\n```\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":611,"cache_read_input_tokens":95572,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":611,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ee6c09f-05f4-414a-b582-02feea8e809f","uuid":"ee0a8a6e-ebd3-49a1-afba-da1dd1efd7e1","timestamp":"2026-07-24T16:48:19.886Z","request_id":"req_011CdMDsn1EYGwpuyFFS2UX1"}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0174ZwRJbDtHgTBGTN1ksuc4","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T163347Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/DEPLOY.md (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ee6c09f-05f4-414a-b582-02feea8e809f","uuid":"e4d9cc7f-fa75-46f3-8c78-c3c772c1e4d7","timestamp":"2026-07-24T16:48:19.901Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T163347Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/DEPLOY.md","content":"# Deploying the Tip Jar site to IPFS\n\nThe frontend ships as a **static export** hosted on IPFS. There is no server —\n`next build` with `output: \"export\"` produces a folder of static files\n(`packages/nextjs/out/`) that any IPFS pinning service can serve.\n\nThis document lists the exact **build**, **upload**, and **post-deploy\nverification** commands. It does **not** upload anything for you.\n\n## 0. Prerequisites\n\n- **Node 20 or 22 LTS** — run `nvm use` (an `.nvmrc` pins `22`). ⚠️ Node ≥ 23\n  defines a global `localStorage` that crashes the static prerender. Verified on\n  Node 22.2.0.\n- `yarn install` has been run.\n- Foundry installed (for the contract deployment step).\n\nThe IPFS build is switched on by `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true`, which makes\n`packages/nextjs/next.config.ts` set:\n\n- `output: \"export\"` — emit a static `out/` folder\n- `trailingSlash: true` — every route becomes `route/index.html` so IPFS gateways resolve directory paths\n- `images.unoptimized: true` — no image optimization server\n\nThe target chain is switched with `NEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK` (see\n`packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`): unset → local Base fork (`chains.foundry`),\n`base` → Base mainnet (`chains.base`).\n\n---\n\n## 1. Deploy the contract to Base mainnet\n\nThe static site reads the TipJar address from\n`packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts`. That file is regenerated per\nnetwork at deploy time, so **you must deploy to Base before building the\nproduction site** — otherwise the build has no `TipJar` entry for chain `8453`.\n\n```bash\n# One-time: create/import a funded deployer keystore\nyarn account:import        # or: yarn generate\n\n# Deploy TipJar (pointed at Base USDC) to Base mainnet\nyarn deploy --network base --keystore <your-keystore>\n```\n\nThis writes the Base (`8453`) `TipJar` entry into `deployedContracts.ts`.\n\nVerify the deployment before building:\n\n```bash\nBASE_RPC=https://mainnet.base.org\nJAR=<address printed by yarn deploy>            # also in deployedContracts.ts under 8453\ncast call $JAR \"usdc()(address)\" --rpc-url $BASE_RPC\n# -> 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913  (Base USDC)\ncast call $JAR \"owner()(address)\" --rpc-url $BASE_RPC\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Build the static site\n\n```bash\nnvm use    # Node 22\n\n# Production build targeting Base mainnet, static-exported for IPFS\nNEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK=base NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true yarn next:build\n```\n\nOutput lands in `packages/nextjs/out/`. Sanity-check it locally before\nuploading:\n\n```bash\n# Every route is a directory with an index.html\nls packages/nextjs/out/tipjar/index.html packages/nextjs/out/index.html\n\n# Serve the exact static bundle and click through /tipjar\nnpx serve packages/nextjs/out\n# open http://localhost:3000/tipjar\n```\n\n> The identical export pipeline is what runs for the local fork build\n> (`NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true yarn next:build`, target unset). It has been\n> verified to emit `/tipjar` as a static page.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Upload to IPFS\n\nPick one. **Run these yourself — this repo does not upload during CI or build.**\n\n### Option A — the built-in `yarn ipfs` script (build + upload via bgipfs)\n\n```bash\n# Builds with NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true and uploads out/ to a public bgipfs gateway.\n# Add NEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK=base to point the built site at Base mainnet.\nNEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK=base yarn ipfs\n# -> prints: 🚀 Upload complete! ... https://community.bgipfs.com/ipfs/<CID>\n```\n\n### Option B — upload the already-built `out/` with the IPFS CLI (Kubo)\n\n```bash\nCID=$(ipfs add -Q -r packages/nextjs/out)\necho \"Root CID: $CID\"\n```\n\n### Option C — a pinning service (Pinata / web3.storage / Filebase)\n\nUpload the `packages/nextjs/out` folder through the service's CLI or dashboard\nand note the returned **root CID**.\n\nWhichever you use, the deliverable is the **root CID** of the `out/` directory.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Post-deploy verification\n\nReplace `<CID>` with the root CID from step 3.\n\n```bash\n# 1. The pinned bundle resolves and the tip jar route exists on a public gateway\ncurl -sfI \"https://ipfs.io/ipfs/<CID>/tipjar/index.html\" | head -1        # -> HTTP/2 200\ncurl -sfI \"https://<CID>.ipfs.dweb.link/tipjar/\"          | head -1        # -> HTTP/2 200\n\n# 2. The HTML actually rendered the app (not an error page)\ncurl -sf \"https://ipfs.io/ipfs/<CID>/tipjar/\" | grep -o \"USDC Tip Jar\"     # -> USDC Tip Jar\n\n# 3. The content is pinned somewhere (survives your local node going away)\nipfs pin ls --type=recursive | grep <CID>                                  # if self-pinning\n#    or confirm the \"pinned\" status in your pinning service dashboard\n\n# 4. Load https://ipfs.io/ipfs/<CID>/tipjar/ in a browser, connect a wallet on\n#    Base, and confirm the tip feed loads and the contract address shown matches\n#    the Base deployment from step 1.\n```\n\nOptionally set a stable name so the CID can change on each redeploy:\n\n```bash\nipfs name publish /ipfs/<CID>        # IPNS\n# or point a DNSLink TXT record: _dnslink.tips.example.com -> dnslink=/ipfs/<CID>\n```\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

It is a compile-time value, so it must be\n  // a single concrete network — the scaffold derives contract types from it.\n  targetNetworks: [\n    chains.foundry\n  ],","originalFile":"import * as chains from \"viem/chains\";\n\n\n\nexport type BaseConfig = {\n  targetNetworks: readonly chains.Chain[];\n  pollingInterval: number;\n  alchemyApiKey: string;\n  rpcOverrides?: Record<number, string>;\n  walletConnectProjectId: string;\n  burnerWalletMode: \"localNetworksOnly\" | \"allNetworks\" | \"disabled\";\n};\n\nexport type ScaffoldConfig = BaseConfig ;\n\nexport const DEFAULT_ALCHEMY_API_KEY = \"IZYEU2cWBgnFmgiTAgpWD\";\n\n// Local dev/demo runs against a Base fork (chain-id 31337 = chains.foundry).\n// The production/IPFS build targets real Base: set NEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK=base.\n// See DEPLOY.md.\nconst targetNetworks = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK === \"base\" ? [chains.base] : [chains.foundry];\n\nconst scaffoldConfig = {\n  // The networks on which your DApp is live\n  targetNetworks,\n  // The interval at which your front-end polls the RPC servers for new data (it has no effect if you only target the local network (default is 4000))\n  pollingInterval: 3000,\n  // This is ours Alchemy's default API key.\n  // You can get your own at https://dashboard.alchemyapi.io\n  // It's recommended to store it in an env variable:\n  // .env.local for local testing, and in the Vercel/system env config for live apps.\n  alchemyApiKey: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_ALCHEMY_API_KEY || DEFAULT_ALCHEMY_API_KEY,\n  // If you want to use a different RPC for a specific network, you can add it here.\n  // The key is the chain ID, and the value is the HTTP RPC URL\n  rpcOverrides: {\n    // Example:\n    // [chains.mainnet.id]: \"https://mainnet.rpc.buidlguidl.com\",\n  },\n  // This is ours WalletConnect's default project ID.\n  // You can get your own at https://cloud.walletconnect.com\n  // It's recommended to store it in an env variable:\n  // .env.local for local testing, and in the Vercel/system env config for live apps.\n  walletConnectProjectId: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_WALLET_CONNECT_PROJECT_ID || '3a8170812b534d0ff9d794f19a901d64',\n  // Configure Burner Wallet visibility:\n  // - \"localNetworksOnly\": only show when all target networks are local (hardhat/anvil)\n  // - \"allNetworks\": show on any configured target networks\n  // - \"disabled\": completely disable\n  burnerWalletMode: 'localNetworksOnly'\n} as const satisfies ScaffoldConfig;\n\nexport default scaffoldConfig;","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":15,"oldLines":14,"newStart":15,"newLines":15,"lines":[" "," export const DEFAULT_ALCHEMY_API_KEY = \"IZYEU2cWBgnFmgiTAgpWD\";"," ","-// Local dev/demo runs against a Base fork (chain-id 31337 = chains.foundry).","-// The production/IPFS build targets real Base: set NEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK=base.","-// See DEPLOY.md.","-const targetNetworks = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK === \"base\" ?
