(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ee6c09f-05f4-414a-b582-02feea8e809f","uuid":"cfaf5a37-8610-4355-a0ea-19d01a3e4a2b","timestamp":"2026-07-24T16:49:31.708Z","tool_use_result":{"filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T163347Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/DEPLOY.md","oldString":"The target chain is switched with `NEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK` (see\n`packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`): unset → local Base fork (`chains.foundry`),\n`base` → Base mainnet (`chains.base`).","newString":"The target chain lives in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts` (`targetNetworks`).\nIt is a compile-time value the scaffold derives contract types from, so it must\nbe a single concrete network — you set it once for the production build (step 2),\nnot via an env var.","originalFile":"# Deploying the Tip Jar site to IPFS\n\nThe frontend ships as a **static export** hosted on IPFS. There is no server —\n`next build` with `output: \"export\"` produces a folder of static files\n(`packages/nextjs/out/`) that any IPFS pinning service can serve.\n\nThis document lists the exact **build**, **upload**, and **post-deploy\nverification** commands. It does **not** upload anything for you.\n\n## 0. Prerequisites\n\n- **Node 20 or 22 LTS** — run `nvm use` (an `.nvmrc` pins `22`). ⚠️ Node ≥ 23\n  defines a global `localStorage` that crashes the static prerender. Verified on\n  Node 22.2.0.\n- `yarn install` has been run.\n- Foundry installed (for the contract deployment step).\n\nThe IPFS build is switched on by `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true`, which makes\n`packages/nextjs/next.config.ts` set:\n\n- `output: \"export\"` — emit a static `out/` folder\n- `trailingSlash: true` — every route becomes `route/index.html` so IPFS gateways resolve directory paths\n- `images.unoptimized: true` — no image optimization server\n\nThe target chain is switched with `NEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK` (see\n`packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`): unset → local Base fork (`chains.foundry`),\n`base` → Base mainnet (`chains.base`).\n\n---\n\n## 1. Deploy the contract to Base mainnet\n\nThe static site reads the TipJar address from\n`packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts`. That file is regenerated per\nnetwork at deploy time, so **you must deploy to Base before building the\nproduction site** — otherwise the build has no `TipJar` entry for chain `8453`.\n\n```bash\n# One-time: create/import a funded deployer keystore\nyarn account:import        # or: yarn generate\n\n# Deploy TipJar (pointed at Base USDC) to Base mainnet\nyarn deploy --network base --keystore <your-keystore>\n```\n\nThis writes the Base (`8453`) `TipJar` entry into `deployedContracts.ts`.\n\nVerify the deployment before building:\n\n```bash\nBASE_RPC=https://mainnet.base.org\nJAR=<address printed by yarn deploy>            # also in deployedContracts.ts under 8453\ncast call $JAR \"usdc()(address)\" --rpc-url $BASE_RPC\n# -> 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913  (Base USDC)\ncast call $JAR \"owner()(address)\" --rpc-url $BASE_RPC\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Build the static site\n\n```bash\nnvm use    # Node 22\n\n# Production build targeting Base mainnet, static-exported for IPFS\nNEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK=base NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true yarn next:build\n```\n\nOutput lands in `packages/nextjs/out/`. Sanity-check it locally before\nuploading:\n\n```bash\n# Every route is a directory with an index.html\nls packages/nextjs/out/tipjar/index.html packages/nextjs/out/index.html\n\n# Serve the exact static bundle and click through /tipjar\nnpx serve packages/nextjs/out\n# open http://localhost:3000/tipjar\n```\n\n> The identical export pipeline is what runs for the local fork build\n> (`NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true yarn next:build`, target unset). It has been\n> verified to emit `/tipjar` as a static page.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Upload to IPFS\n\nPick one. **Run these yourself — this repo does not upload during CI or build.**\n\n### Option A — the built-in `yarn ipfs` script (build + upload via bgipfs)\n\n```bash\n# Builds with NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true and uploads out/ to a public bgipfs gateway.\n# Add NEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK=base to point the built site at Base mainnet.\nNEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK=base yarn ipfs\n# -> prints: 🚀 Upload complete! ... https://community.bgipfs.com/ipfs/<CID>\n```\n\n### Option B — upload the already-built `out/` with the IPFS CLI (Kubo)\n\n```bash\nCID=$(ipfs add -Q -r packages/nextjs/out)\necho \"Root CID: $CID\"\n```\n\n### Option C — a pinning service (Pinata / web3.storage / Filebase)\n\nUpload the `packages/nextjs/out` folder through the service's CLI or dashboard\nand note the returned **root CID**.\n\nWhichever you use, the deliverable is the **root CID** of the `out/` directory.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Post-deploy verification\n\nReplace `<CID>` with the root CID from step 3.\n\n```bash\n# 1. The pinned bundle resolves and the tip jar route exists on a public gateway\ncurl -sfI \"https://ipfs.io/ipfs/<CID>/tipjar/index.html\" | head -1        # -> HTTP/2 200\ncurl -sfI \"https://<CID>.ipfs.dweb.link/tipjar/\"          | head -1        # -> HTTP/2 200\n\n# 2. The HTML actually rendered the app (not an error page)\ncurl -sf \"https://ipfs.io/ipfs/<CID>/tipjar/\" | grep -o \"USDC Tip Jar\"     # -> USDC Tip Jar\n\n# 3. The content is pinned somewhere (survives your local node going away)\nipfs pin ls --type=recursive | grep <CID>                                  # if self-pinning\n#    or confirm the \"pinned\" status in your pinning service dashboard\n\n# 4. Load https://ipfs.io/ipfs/<CID>/tipjar/ in a browser, connect a wallet on\n#    Base, and confirm the tip feed loads and the contract address shown matches\n#    the Base deployment from step 1.\n```\n\nOptionally set a stable name so the CID can change on each redeploy:\n\n```bash\nipfs name publish /ipfs/<CID>        # IPNS\n# or point a DNSLink TXT record: _dnslink.tips.example.com -> dnslink=/ipfs/<CID>\n```\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":22,"oldLines":9,"newStart":22,"newLines":10,"lines":[" - `trailingSlash: true` — every route becomes `route/index.html` so IPFS gateways resolve directory paths"," - `images.unoptimized: true` — no image optimization server"," ","-The target chain is switched with `NEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK` (see","-`packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`): unset → local Base fork (`chains.foundry`),","-`base` → Base mainnet (`chains.base`).","+The target chain lives in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts` (`targetNetworks`).","+It is a compile-time value the scaffold derives contract types from, so it must","+be a single concrete network — you set it once for the production build (step 2),","+not via an env var."," "," ---"," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

Sanity-check it locally before\nuploading:","originalFile":"# Deploying the Tip Jar site to IPFS\n\nThe frontend ships as a **static export** hosted on IPFS. There is no server —\n`next build` with `output: \"export\"` produces a folder of static files\n(`packages/nextjs/out/`) that any IPFS pinning service can serve.\n\nThis document lists the exact **build**, **upload**, and **post-deploy\nverification** commands. It does **not** upload anything for you.\n\n## 0. Prerequisites\n\n- **Node 20 or 22 LTS** — run `nvm use` (an `.nvmrc` pins `22`). ⚠️ Node ≥ 23\n  defines a global `localStorage` that crashes the static prerender. Verified on\n  Node 22.2.0.\n- `yarn install` has been run.\n- Foundry installed (for the contract deployment step).\n\nThe IPFS build is switched on by `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true`, which makes\n`packages/nextjs/next.config.ts` set:\n\n- `output: \"export\"` — emit a static `out/` folder\n- `trailingSlash: true` — every route becomes `route/index.html` so IPFS gateways resolve directory paths\n- `images.unoptimized: true` — no image optimization server\n\nThe target chain lives in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts` (`targetNetworks`).\nIt is a compile-time value the scaffold derives contract types from, so it must\nbe a single concrete network — you set it once for the production build (step 2),\nnot via an env var.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Deploy the contract to Base mainnet\n\nThe static site reads the TipJar address from\n`packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts`. That file is regenerated per\nnetwork at deploy time, so **you must deploy to Base before building the\nproduction site** — otherwise the build has no `TipJar` entry for chain `8453`.\n\n```bash\n# One-time: create/import a funded deployer keystore\nyarn account:import        # or: yarn generate\n\n# Deploy TipJar (pointed at Base USDC) to Base mainnet\nyarn deploy --network base --keystore <your-keystore>\n```\n\nThis writes the Base (`8453`) `TipJar` entry into `deployedContracts.ts`.\n\nVerify the deployment before building:\n\n```bash\nBASE_RPC=https://mainnet.base.org\nJAR=<address printed by yarn deploy>            # also in deployedContracts.ts under 8453\ncast call $JAR \"usdc()(address)\" --rpc-url $BASE_RPC\n# -> 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913  (Base USDC)\ncast call $JAR \"owner()(address)\" --rpc-url $BASE_RPC\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Build the static site\n\n```bash\nnvm use    # Node 22\n\n# Production build targeting Base mainnet, static-exported for IPFS\nNEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK=base NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true yarn next:build\n```\n\nOutput lands in `packages/nextjs/out/`. Sanity-check it locally before\nuploading:\n\n```bash\n# Every route is a directory with an index.html\nls packages/nextjs/out/tipjar/index.html packages/nextjs/out/index.html\n\n# Serve the exact static bundle and click through /tipjar\nnpx serve packages/nextjs/out\n# open http://localhost:3000/tipjar\n```\n\n> The identical export pipeline is what runs for the local fork build\n> (`NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true yarn next:build`, target unset). It has been\n> verified to emit `/tipjar` as a static page.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Upload to IPFS\n\nPick one. **Run these yourself — this repo does not upload during CI or build.**\n\n### Option A — the built-in `yarn ipfs` script (build + upload via bgipfs)\n\n```bash\n# Builds with NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true and uploads out/ to a public bgipfs gateway.\n# Add NEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK=base to point the built site at Base mainnet.\nNEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK=base yarn ipfs\n# -> prints: 🚀 Upload complete! ... https://community.bgipfs.com/ipfs/<CID>\n```\n\n### Option B — upload the already-built `out/` with the IPFS CLI (Kubo)\n\n```bash\nCID=$(ipfs add -Q -r packages/nextjs/out)\necho \"Root CID: $CID\"\n```\n\n### Option C — a pinning service (Pinata / web3.storage / Filebase)\n\nUpload the `packages/nextjs/out` folder through the service's CLI or dashboard\nand note the returned **root CID**.\n\nWhichever you use, the deliverable is the **root CID** of the `out/` directory.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Post-deploy verification\n\nReplace `<CID>` with the root CID from step 3.\n\n```bash\n# 1. The pinned bundle resolves and the tip jar route exists on a public gateway\ncurl -sfI \"https://ipfs.io/ipfs/<CID>/tipjar/index.html\" | head -1        # -> HTTP/2 200\ncurl -sfI \"https://<CID>.ipfs.dweb.link/tipjar/\"          | head -1        # -> HTTP/2 200\n\n# 2. The HTML actually rendered the app (not an error page)\ncurl -sf \"https://ipfs.io/ipfs/<CID>/tipjar/\" | grep -o \"USDC Tip Jar\"     # -> USDC Tip Jar\n\n# 3. The content is pinned somewhere (survives your local node going away)\nipfs pin ls --type=recursive | grep <CID>                                  # if self-pinning\n#    or confirm the \"pinned\" status in your pinning service dashboard\n\n# 4. Load https://ipfs.io/ipfs/<CID>/tipjar/ in a browser, connect a wallet on\n#    Base, and confirm the tip feed loads and the contract address shown matches\n#    the Base deployment from step 1.\n```\n\nOptionally set a stable name so the CID can change on each redeploy:\n\n```bash\nipfs name publish /ipfs/<CID>        # IPNS\n# or point a DNSLink TXT record: _dnslink.tips.example.com -> dnslink=/ipfs/<CID>\n```\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":60,"oldLines":11,"newStart":60,"newLines":20,"lines":[" "," ## 2. Post-deploy verification\n\nReplace `<CID>` with the root CID from step 3.\n\n```bash\n# 1. The pinned bundle resolves and the tip jar route exists on a public gateway\ncurl -sfI \"https://ipfs.io/ipfs/<CID>/tipjar/index.html\" | head -1        # -> HTTP/2 200\ncurl -sfI \"https://<CID>.ipfs.dweb.link/tipjar/\"          | head -1        # -> HTTP/2 200\n\n# 2. The HTML actually rendered the app (not an error page)\ncurl -sf \"https://ipfs.io/ipfs/<CID>/tipjar/\" | grep -o \"USDC Tip Jar\"     # -> USDC Tip Jar\n\n# 3. The content is pinned somewhere (survives your local node going away)\nipfs pin ls --type=recursive | grep <CID>                                  # if self-pinning\n#    or confirm the \"pinned\" status in your pinning service dashboard\n\n# 4. Load https://ipfs.io/ipfs/<CID>/tipjar/ in a browser, connect a wallet on\n#    Base, and confirm the tip feed loads and the contract address shown matches\n#    the Base deployment from step 1.\n```\n\nOptionally set a stable name so the CID can change on each redeploy:\n\n```bash\nipfs name publish /ipfs/<CID>        # IPNS\n# or point a DNSLink TXT record: _dnslink.tips.example.com -> dnslink=/ipfs/<CID>\n```\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":89,"oldLines":8,"newStart":89,"newLines":8,"lines":[" ```"," "," > The identical export pipeline is what runs for the local fork build","-> (`NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true yarn next:build`, target unset). It has been verified to emit `/tipjar` as a static page."," "," ---"," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HuTLaeSPhZxJXqmvMQ3upM","type":"tool_result","content":"The file /Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T163347Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/DEPLOY.md has been updated successfully. (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ee6c09f-05f4-414a-b582-02feea8e809f","uuid":"57ff65d6-7da1-4350-aa54-ae39f0628d8b","timestamp":"2026-07-24T16:49:50.252Z","tool_use_result":{"filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T163347Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/DEPLOY.md","oldString":"```bash\n# Builds with NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true and uploads out/ to a public bgipfs gateway.\n# Add NEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK=base to point the built site at Base mainnet.\nNEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK=base yarn ipfs\n# -> prints: 🚀 Upload complete! ... https://community.bgipfs.com/ipfs/<CID>\n```","newString":"```bash\n# With targetNetworks set to [chains.base] (step 2), this builds with\n# NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true and uploads out/ to a public bgipfs gateway.\nyarn ipfs\n# -> prints: 🚀 Upload complete! ... https://community.bgipfs.com/ipfs/<CID>\n```","originalFile":"# Deploying the Tip Jar site to IPFS\n\nThe frontend ships as a **static export** hosted on IPFS. There is no server —\n`next build` with `output: \"export\"` produces a folder of static files\n(`packages/nextjs/out/`) that any IPFS pinning service can serve.\n\nThis document lists the exact **build**, **upload**, and **post-deploy\nverification** commands. It does **not** upload anything for you.\n\n## 0. Prerequisites\n\n- **Node 20 or 22 LTS** — run `nvm use` (an `.nvmrc` pins `22`). ⚠️ Node ≥ 23\n  defines a global `localStorage` that crashes the static prerender. Verified on\n  Node 22.2.0.\n- `yarn install` has been run.\n- Foundry installed (for the contract deployment step).\n\nThe IPFS build is switched on by `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true`, which makes\n`packages/nextjs/next.config.ts` set:\n\n- `output: \"export\"` — emit a static `out/` folder\n- `trailingSlash: true` — every route becomes `route/index.html` so IPFS gateways resolve directory paths\n- `images.unoptimized: true` — no image optimization server\n\nThe target chain lives in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts` (`targetNetworks`).\nIt is a compile-time value the scaffold derives contract types from, so it must\nbe a single concrete network — you set it once for the production build (step 2),\nnot via an env var.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Deploy the contract to Base mainnet\n\nThe static site reads the TipJar address from\n`packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts`. That file is regenerated per\nnetwork at deploy time, so **you must deploy to Base before building the\nproduction site** — otherwise the build has no `TipJar` entry for chain `8453`.\n\n```bash\n# One-time: create/import a funded deployer keystore\nyarn account:import        # or: yarn generate\n\n# Deploy TipJar (pointed at Base USDC) to Base mainnet\nyarn deploy --network base --keystore <your-keystore>\n```\n\nThis writes the Base (`8453`) `TipJar` entry into `deployedContracts.ts`.\n\nVerify the deployment before building:\n\n```bash\nBASE_RPC=https://mainnet.base.org\nJAR=<address printed by yarn deploy>            # also in deployedContracts.ts under 8453\ncast call $JAR \"usdc()(address)\" --rpc-url $BASE_RPC\n# -> 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913  (Base USDC)\ncast call $JAR \"owner()(address)\" --rpc-url $BASE_RPC\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Build the static site\n\nFirst point the frontend at Base mainnet. In\n`packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts` set:\n\n```ts\ntargetNetworks: [chains.base],   // was [chains.foundry] for local demos\n```\n\nThen build:\n\n```bash\nnvm use    # Node 22\n\n# Static export for IPFS (output: \"export\")\nNEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true yarn next:build\n```\n\nOutput lands in `packages/nextjs/out/`. Sanity-check it locally before\nuploading:\n\n```bash\n# Every route is a directory with an index.html\nls packages/nextjs/out/tipjar/index.html packages/nextjs/out/index.html\n\n# Serve the exact static bundle and click through /tipjar\nnpx serve packages/nextjs/out\n# open http://localhost:3000/tipjar\n```\n\n> The identical export pipeline is what runs for the local fork build\n> (`targetNetworks: [chains.foundry]` + `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true yarn\n> next:build`). It has been verified to emit `/tipjar` as a static page.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Upload to IPFS\n\nPick one. **Run these yourself — this repo does not upload during CI or build.**\n\n### Option A — the built-in `yarn ipfs` script (build + upload via bgipfs)\n\n```bash\n# Builds with NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true and uploads out/ to a public bgipfs gateway.\n# Add NEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK=base to point the built site at Base mainnet.\nNEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK=base yarn ipfs\n# -> prints: 🚀 Upload complete! ... https://community.bgipfs.com/ipfs/<CID>\n```\n\n### Option B — upload the already-built `out/` with the IPFS CLI (Kubo)\n\n```bash\nCID=$(ipfs add -Q -r packages/nextjs/out)\necho \"Root CID: $CID\"\n```\n\n### Option C — a pinning service (Pinata / web3.storage / Filebase)\n\nUpload the `packages/nextjs/out` folder through the service's CLI or dashboard\nand note the returned **root CID**.\n\nWhichever you use, the deliverable is the **root CID** of the `out/` directory.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Post-deploy verification\n\nReplace `<CID>` with the root CID from step 3.\n\n```bash\n# 1. The pinned bundle resolves and the tip jar route exists on a public gateway\ncurl -sfI \"https://ipfs.io/ipfs/<CID>/tipjar/index.html\" | head -1        # -> HTTP/2 200\ncurl -sfI \"https://<CID>.ipfs.dweb.link/tipjar/\"          | head -1        # -> HTTP/2 200\n\n# 2. The HTML actually rendered the app (not an error page)\ncurl -sf \"https://ipfs.io/ipfs/<CID>/tipjar/\" | grep -o \"USDC Tip Jar\"     # -> USDC Tip Jar\n\n# 3. The content is pinned somewhere (survives your local node going away)\nipfs pin ls --type=recursive | grep <CID>                                  # if self-pinning\n#    or confirm the \"pinned\" status in your pinning service dashboard\n\n# 4. Load https://ipfs.io/ipfs/<CID>/tipjar/ in a browser, connect a wallet on\n#    Base, and confirm the tip feed loads and the contract address shown matches\n#    the Base deployment from step 1.\n```\n\nOptionally set a stable name so the CID can change on each redeploy:\n\n```bash\nipfs name publish /ipfs/<CID>        # IPNS\n# or point a DNSLink TXT record: _dnslink.tips.example.com -> dnslink=/ipfs/<CID>\n```\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":101,"oldLines":9,"newStart":101,"newLines":9,"lines":[" ### Option A — the built-in `yarn ipfs` script (build + upload via bgipfs)"," "," ```bash","-# Builds with NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true and uploads out/ to a public bgipfs gateway.","-# Add NEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK=base to point the built site at Base mainnet.","-NEXT_PUBLIC_TARGET_NETWORK=base yarn ipfs","+# With targetNetworks set to [chains.base] (step 2), this builds with","+# NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true and uploads out/ to a public bgipfs gateway.","+yarn ipfs"," # -> prints: 🚀 Upload complete! ... https://community.bgipfs.com/ipfs/<CID>"," ```"," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMEBZjJDMa37PDTQD5Mo","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01LxAtnispnzCsA91eQmDte4","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/.claude/projects/-Users-spencerfaber-dev-ethskills-evals/memory/se2-node-static-export.md","content":"---\nname: se2-node-static-export\ndescription: Scaffold-ETH 2 Next.js static export (IPFS build) crashes on Node >=23 due to a global localStorage\nmetadata:\n  type: project\n---\n\nIn the ethskills-evals SE-2 workspaces, `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true yarn next:build`\n(Next.js static `output: \"export\"`) fails prerendering the stock `/_not-found`\npage with `TypeError: localStorage.getItem is not a function`.\n\n**Cause:** Node >=23 (env default here is 25.9.0) exposes a global `localStorage`\nobject; wagmi/burner-connector code that guards with `typeof localStorage !==\n\"undefined\"` then runs during SSR prerender and blows up. Node 20/22 have no\nglobal `localStorage`, so the guard works.\n\n**Fix:** build with Node 20 or 22 LTS (`nvm use`, e.g. 22.2.0 is installed and\nverified). Do NOT bake `--no-experimental-webstorage` into NODE_OPTIONS — that\nflag errors on Node <22.4.\n\nAlso: `scaffold.config.ts` `targetNetworks` must be a single concrete network\n(compile-time value the contract types derive from). A runtime env ternary makes\nit a union type and collapses `ContractName` to the intersection of both\nnetworks' contracts, breaking type-check.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":558,"cache_read_input_tokens":112265,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":558,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":50,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ee6c09f-05f4-414a-b582-02feea8e809f","uuid":"8f6d5ca5-c728-47ea-882a-901eaebbf8f6","timestamp":"2026-07-24T16:51:28.056Z","request_id":"req_011CdMEBYAnTpCiLi5q9uqEg"}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01LxAtnispnzCsA91eQmDte4","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /Users/spencerfaber/.claude/projects/-Users-spencerfaber-dev-ethskills-evals/memory/se2-node-static-export.md"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ee6c09f-05f4-414a-b582-02feea8e809f","uuid":"8498204d-8c36-4b1d-9274-3748c41145f0","timestamp":"2026-07-24T16:51:28.071Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/.claude/projects/-Users-spencerfaber-dev-ethskills-evals/memory/se2-node-static-export.md","content":"---\nname: se2-node-static-export\ndescription: Scaffold-ETH 2 Next.js static export (IPFS build) crashes on Node >=23 due to a global localStorage\nmetadata: \n  node_type: memory\n  type: project\n  originSessionId: 2ee6c09f-05f4-414a-b582-02feea8e809f\n  modified: 2026-07-24T16:51:28.063Z\n---\n\nIn the ethskills-evals SE-2 workspaces, `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true yarn next:build`\n(Next.js static `output: \"export\"`) fails prerendering the stock `/_not-found`\npage with `TypeError: localStorage.getItem is not a function`.\n\n**Cause:** Node >=23 (env default here is 25.9.0) exposes a global `localStorage`\nobject; wagmi/burner-connector code that guards with `typeof localStorage !==\n\"undefined\"` then runs during SSR prerender and blows up. Node 20/22 have no\nglobal `localStorage`, so the guard works.\n\n**Fix:** build with Node 20 or 22 LTS (`nvm use`, e.g. 22.2.0 is installed and\nverified). Do NOT bake `--no-experimental-webstorage` into NODE_OPTIONS — that\nflag errors on Node <22.4.\n\nAlso: `scaffold.config.ts` `targetNetworks` must be a single concrete network\n(compile-time value the contract types derive from). A runtime env ternary makes\nit a union type and collapses `ContractName` to the intersection of both\nnetworks' contracts, breaking type-check.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false,"memdirStamped":true}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMECqLRtsWj88oFL2HZY","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. Here's what I built and verified.\n\n## USDC Tip Jar for Base\n\n**Contract** — `packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol`\n- Accepts USDC tips via `tip(amount, message)` (pulls USDC with `transferFrom` + SafeERC20; measures actual received amount), stores an on-chain feed (`from`, `amount`, `timestamp`, `message`), tracks `totalTipped`/`tippedBy`, emits `NewTip`, and lets the owner `withdraw()`. Uses OpenZeppelin `Ownable` + `ReentrancyGuard`.\n- The Base USDC address is injected at deploy time, so the identical contract works on a local Base fork and on Base mainnet.\n- Deploy script `DeployTipJar.s.sol` wired into `Deploy.s.sol`.\n- `test/TipJar.t.sol`: **8 tests pass** (balance movement, feed/event, zero-amount & no-approval reverts, newest-first pagination, owner-only withdraw).\n\n**Frontend** — `packages/nextjs/app/tipjar/`\n- Tip feed (`useScaffoldEventHistory` on `NewTip`), stats (total tipped / count), and an approve→tip form with USDC balance display and input validation. USDC added to `externalContracts.ts` for chains 31337 and 8453; nav link added. Type-checks clean and static-exports `/tipjar` with content.\n\n**Docs**\n- `README.md` — exact local workflow: `yarn fork base` → `yarn deploy` → seed test identities with USDC by impersonating Circle's `masterMinter` (`scripts-js/seed-usdc.sh`) → `yarn start` → demo a tip between Alice/Bob. No real money at risk (all fork state).\n- `DEPLOY.md` — exact IPFS build (`NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true`), upload (bgipfs / Kubo / pinning service), and post-deploy verification commands. Nothing was uploaded.\n- `.nvmrc` pins Node 22.\n\n## What I verified end-to-end against the running Base fork\n- Deployed TipJar; confirmed it points at real Base USDC and is owned by the deployer.\n- Minted USDC to a test identity, ran **approve → tip**: 5 USDC moved from Alice (100→95) into the jar, `totalTipped`/`tipCount`/feed all updated, then owner `withdraw()` pulled the 5 USDC out.\n- Full IPFS static export produces `out/tipjar/index.html` containing the rendered app.\n\n## One thing to flag\nThe static export **must be built with Node 20/22**, not the environment's default Node 25.9.0 — Node ≥23 exposes a global `localStorage` that crashes Next's prerender of the stock pages. This is documented in both `.nvmrc` and the docs. Also, for a real production build you set `targetNetworks: [chains.base]` in `scaffold.config.ts` after deploying to Base (it's a compile-time value); I couldn't execute a real mainnet deploy (real funds), so that path is documented rather than run."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":271,"cache_read_input_tokens":113481,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":271,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2ee6c09f-05f4-414a-b582-02feea8e809f","uuid":"fc323ceb-27f3-4492-bd56-7c0854574208","timestamp":"2026-07-24T16:51:48.220Z","request_id":"req_011CdMECmXjfJMsYdAA1VSwg"}

Here's what I built and verified.\n\n## USDC Tip Jar for Base\n\n**Contract** — `packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol`\n- Accepts USDC tips via `tip(amount, message)` (pulls USDC with `transferFrom` + SafeERC20; measures actual received amount), stores an on-chain feed (`from`, `amount`, `timestamp`, `message`), tracks `totalTipped`/`tippedBy`, emits `NewTip`, and lets the owner `withdraw()`. Uses OpenZeppelin `Ownable` + `ReentrancyGuard`.\n- The Base USDC address is injected at deploy time, so the identical contract works on a local Base fork and on Base mainnet.\n- Deploy script `DeployTipJar.s.sol` wired into `Deploy.s.sol`.\n- `test/TipJar.t.sol`: **8 tests pass** (balance movement, feed/event, zero-amount & no-approval reverts, newest-first pagination, owner-only withdraw).\n\n**Frontend** — `packages/nextjs/app/tipjar/`\n- Tip feed (`useScaffoldEventHistory` on `NewTip`), stats (total tipped / count), and an approve→tip form with USDC balance display and input validation. USDC added to `externalContracts.ts` for chains 31337 and 8453; nav link added. Type-checks clean and static-exports `/tipjar` with content.\n\n**Docs**\n- `README.md` — exact local workflow: `yarn fork base` → `yarn deploy` → seed test identities with USDC by impersonating Circle's `masterMinter` (`scripts-js/seed-usdc.sh`) → `yarn start` → demo a tip between Alice/Bob. No real money at risk (all fork state).\n- `DEPLOY.md` — exact IPFS build (`NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true`), upload (bgipfs / Kubo / pinning service), and post-deploy verification commands. Nothing was uploaded.\n- `.nvmrc` pins Node 22.\n\n## What I verified end-to-end against the running Base fork\n- Deployed TipJar; confirmed it points at real Base USDC and is owned by the deployer.\n- Minted USDC to a test identity, ran **approve → tip**: 5 USDC moved from Alice (100→95) into the jar, `totalTipped`/`tipCount`/feed all updated, then owner `withdraw()` pulled the 5 USDC out.\n- Full IPFS static export produces `out/tipjar/index.html` containing the rendered app.\n\n## One thing to flag\nThe static export **must be built with Node 20/22**, not the environment's default Node 25.9.0 — Node ≥23 exposes a global `localStorage` that crashes Next's prerender of the stock pages. This is documented in both `.nvmrc` and the docs. Also, for a real production build you set `targetNetworks: [chains.base]` in `scaffold.config.ts` after deploying to Base (it's a compile-time value); I couldn't execute a real mainnet deploy (real funds), so that path is documented rather than run.","ttft_ms":3278,"ttft_stream_ms":1231,"time_to_request_ms":113,"type":"result","duration_ms":1080552,"uuid":"cac2a187-7d69-46e3-a8db-5f4e61aee7c3"}
